- **Vite 8.1.2** (June 30, 2026 patch — pure bug-fix release that supersedes 8.1.1; **8.1.2 is the recommended Vite version** because it: (1) reverts the es-module-lexer 2.2.0 upgrade in 8.1.1 (PR #22827) which broke any prod output chunk >~4 MB with `WebAssembly.Memory.grow()` error ([guybedford/es-module-lexer#214](https://github.com/guybedford/es-module-lexer/issues/214), reported as Vite [#22826](https://github.com/vitejs/vite/issues/22826)); (2) reverts PR #22687 multi-null-byte id escape from 8.1.1 because it broke the **waku** and **vite-plugin-rsc** ecosystem-ci suites; (3) re-applies the 8.1.1 pnpm `.modules.yaml` workspace-root resolution (PR #22825, with a corrected implementation of the originally-intended #22757); and (4) retains all the 8.1.1-intended fixes (`resolve.tsconfigPaths` CSS/Sass regression fix #22775, Bundled Dev Mode `import.meta.hot.invalidate()` stack overflow #22797, Bundled Dev Mode HMR/lazy-compile asset serving #22745, `esbuild.jsxSideEffects` → `oxc.jsx.pure` inversion #22809, `indexHtmlMiddleware` malformed URI crash #22781, missing sourcemap field in plugins #22782, `ERR_CLOSED_SERVER` in optimize-deps scanner #22784). **Skip 8.1.1 entirely** — go 8.1.0 → 8.1.2. See setup.md → Vite 8.1.2 for the long-form patterns, the `tsconfig.json` `include` requirement for CSS/Sass files, and the 8.1.1/8.1.2/es-module-lexer migration matrix)
- **@biomejs/biome 2.5.2** (recommended linter/formatter — 10–100x faster than ESLint, v2 has breaking changes from v1; run `npx biome migrate --write` after every upgrade)
- **TanStack Query v5.101.2** (React Query v5 — gcTime replaces cacheTime, improved SSR hydration, `skipToken` for dependent queries; **5.101.2 (June 27, 2026)** patches `@tanstack/query-devtools` — `setupStyleSheet` now sets `window.__nonce__` when `styleNonce` is passed ([#10736](https://github.com/TanStack/query/pull/10736), fixes a silent CSP violation where the devtools' `<style>` tag was injected without a nonce and tripped strict `style-src 'nonce-...'` policies in production), `PiPContext.closePipWindow` now resets `pip_open` in localStorage to stop auto-reopen ([#10813](https://github.com/TanStack/query/pull/10813)), `setupStyleSheet` dedup scoped to shadow-DOM target ([#10815](https://github.com/TanStack/query/pull/10815)), `last-updated` sort returns 0 on ties so the comparator is deterministic ([#10812](https://github.com/TanStack/query/pull/10812)), Theme sub-trigger className typo `position` → `theme` ([#10811](https://github.com/TanStack/query/pull/10811)))
- **Vitest 4.1.9** (Browser Mode stable, Visual Regression testing via `toMatchScreenshot`, Playwright Trace support; requires Vite ≥ 6 + Node.js ≥ 20; **≥ 4.1.8 required for the CDP RCE fix (GHSA-g8mr-85jm-7xhm, CVSS 9.8)**; use `api.allowWrite: false, api.allowExec: false` in CI; **Vitest 5.0 in beta** — three beta releases between May 19 and June 15, 2026 (beta.3, beta.4, beta.5) introduced hard requirement Node 22 + Vite 6.4, strict `toHaveTextContent` + new `toMatchTextContent`, Browser Mode `locators.exact: true` default, `expect.poll` fails on timeout, hoistable-methods-out-of-scope throw, removed entry points (`vitest/coverage` → `vitest/node`, `vitest/environments` / `vitest/snapshot` → `vitest/runtime`, `vitest/runners` / `vitest/suite` → `TestRunner` from `vitest`, `vitest/mocker` → `@vitest/mocker`), `bench` moved into `test()` fixture, `test.sequential` → `{ concurrent: false }`, no ancestor-directory config lookup, `@vitest/runner` inlined into `vitest`, `TestModule.id` 1-based, `coverage.thresholds.perFile` accepts an object, browser orchestrator requires `sessionId`)
- **@playwright/test 1.61.1** (E2E — **1.61.0 (June 15, 2026)** shipped WebAuthn passkeys virtual authenticator via `browserContext.credentials.create/install`, `page.localStorage` / `page.sessionStorage` (origin-aware, replaces `page.evaluate('localStorage…')` boilerplate), `apiResponse.securityDetails()` / `serverAddr()` network parity with browser responses, `testOptions.video` modes parity with trace (`'on-all-retries'`, `'retain-on-first-failure'`, `'retain-on-failure-and-retries'`), `expect.soft.poll(...)`, `fullConfig.argv`, `fullConfig.failOnFlakyTests`, `testInfo.errors` AggregateError sub-entries, `-G` shorthand for `--grep-invert`, HAR + trace now include WebSocket frames, Ubuntu 26.04 host support, browsers Chromium 149.0.7827.55 / Firefox 151.0 / WebKit 26.5; **1.61.1 (June 23, 2026)** fixed five 1.61 regressions — `expect.extend` matcher-name shadowing built-ins, UI-mode API request byte count mismatch, trace-viewer WebSocket timestamps scaled by 1/1000, sync ESM loader crash on Node 22.15, pnpm workspace symlink `.ts` subpath resolution)
- **React Hook Form v7.80.0** + **@hookform/resolvers v5.4.0** (7.80.0 ships per-field `disabled` on `useFieldArray` items; broad perf pass; fixes `[]` vs `{}` `deepEqual` regression from 7.79.0) (compatible with Zod v4; v8.0.0-beta available with `createForm` API)
- **Node.js 24 LTS** (Node.js 22 LTS also supported)

## Pro Tips

- Use `create-next-app --typescript --tailwind --eslint --app --src-dir --import-alias "@/*"` for a fully typed Next.js project
- Run `npx tsc --noEmit` to type-check without building
- Use `shadcn@latest add <component>` to add components — they live in your codebase, not node_modules
- Use `next.config.ts` for all Next.js configuration (not `.js`)
- Use `react-hook-form` + `zod` for all forms — never manage form state with useState
- Enable React Compiler in `next.config.ts` with `reactCompiler: true` — eliminates most `useMemo`/`useCallback` manually
- Use Biome (`npx biome check`) for linting — 10–100x faster than ESLint
- Run `npx biome migrate --write` after every Biome upgrade to migrate your config for breaking changes
- Use `use cache` for all server-side data fetching in Next.js 16 — it's the explicit opt-in caching model

Start with `setup.md` to initialize a project, then `components.md` for shadcn/ui patterns.

## Next.js 16 Migration Notes

Key breaking changes from Next.js 15 → 16:

| Change | Impact |
|---|---|
| **`use cache` directive** replaces `unstable_cache` | New explicit caching API — mark data functions with `use cache` + `cacheTag` |
| **Implicit caching removed** — everything is dynamic by default | Old `fetch` caching patterns deprecated; use `use cache` explicitly |
| **PPR (Partial Prerendering) stable** — `cacheComponents: true` | Static shell + streaming dynamic content; no longer experimental |
| **Router scroll optimization** enabled by default | Previously scroll was reset on navigation; now preserved by default |
| Flat config default in `@next/eslint-plugin-next` | ESLint config format change |
| Deprecated `.turbo` config object removed | Use `turbopack` key in `next.config.ts` instead |
| `publicRuntimeConfig` / `serverRuntimeConfig` removed | Use environment variables directly |
| **`next lint` removed** | Use Biome (`npx biome check`) or ESLint (`npx eslint .`) directly |
| **`middleware.ts` deprecated → `proxy.ts`** | `middleware` export renamed to `proxy`; must be `async`; `matcher` moved to `next.config.ts` or exported from `proxy.ts` |

**Upgrade:** `npx @next/codemod@canary upgrade latest` (Next.js 16.2+) — automated upgrade CLI that handles codemods, config migrations, and breaking changes in one command. **In canary.72+ (PR #95312, June 30, 2026) accepts `-y, --yes` for agents/CI** — auto-enabled when stdin is not a TTY so AI coding agents no longer hang on the recommended-codemods multiselect. Alternatively `npx next upgrade` (interactive, Next.js 16.1+) or `npm install next@latest`.

## 1.4.77 — 2026-07-21T18:03Z

**JULY 2026 SECURITY RELEASE — ALL 9 CVEs NOW DOCUMENTED.** The July 21, 2026 security release shipped at ~16:00 UTC (16.2.11 / 15.5.21). Full CVE table added to `security.md`: 4 High (DoS via Server Actions, Turbopack middleware bypass with single i18n locale, SSRF via rewrites, SSRF via Server Actions on custom servers) + 5 Medium (Image Optimization SVG DoS, unbounded Edge payload, Server Function endpoint disclosure, cache confusion ×2). The live status checkpoints (T-24h through T+6h) removed and replaced with final resolution. `api.md` gained "Server Action Security Hardening (canary.92)" section covering 7 PRs: bodySizeLimit Edge enforcement, correct origin for custom-server redirects, exotic rewrite param encoding, server reference ID validation (closes CVE-2026-64643), basePath preservation in redirects, and two fetch cache key fixes (effective-request keying + byte-exact binary body hashing). `deployment.md` gained "July 2026 Security Releases" section documenting 16.2.11 (Active LTS) + 15.5.21 (Maintenance LTS) + canary.92 upgrade path, post-upgrade checklist, and the monthly cadence reminder (next: August 20, 2026). canary.92 non-security items: Turbopack middleware i18n single-locale fix, MPA form validation perf, SVG detectContentType perf, instant validation navigation fix, `use cache` error handling, docs AI agents guide expansion. React 19.2.8 (2026-07-21) — performance improvements when decoding RSC (PR #37087, eps1lon). TanStack Query 5.101.4 (2026-07-21) — patch on query-core 5.101.4.

Sources:
- [Next.js blog: July 2026 Security Release](https://nextjs.org/blog/july-2026-security-release)
- [GitHub: v16.2.11](https://github.com/vercel/next.js/releases/tag/v16.2.11) · [v15.5.21](https://github.com/vercel/next.js/releases/tag/v15.5.21) · [v16.3.0-canary.92](https://github.com/vercel/next.js/releases/tag/v16.3.0-canary.92)
- [React v19.2.8](https://github.com/facebook/react/releases/tag/v19.2.8) · [TanStack Query 5.101.4](https://github.com/TanStack/query/releases/tag/release-2026-07-21-1305)

## Critical Rules (Never Forget)

- **`'use client'` boundary** — Only add when you need browser APIs, hooks, or interactivity. Keep everything else server-side.
- **Server Components can't use hooks** — No `useState`, `useEffect`, `useContext` in server components.
- **Colocate data fetching** — Fetch data in the component that needs it, not prop-drill from the top.
- **Zod for all schema validation** — Never trust raw form/API data without validation.
- **Tailwind = utility-first** — Don't fight it. Compose utilities, don't fight the cascade.
- **Server Actions = mutations** — Use them for form submits and data mutations; fetch for reads.
- **`<Image>` over `<img>`** — Always use Next's Image for automatic optimization and CLS prevention.
- **Abort in-flight requests** — Use `AbortController` to prevent race conditions on navigation.
- **Types in, types out** — Mirror backend response types exactly. No `any` from API calls.

## Version Defaults

^- **Next.js 16.2.10** (latest stable — App Router, Server Components, Server Actions, Turbopack stable for production, Node.js proxy, `use cache` directive, PPR stable; **16.3.0-canary.72** released June 30, 2026 ([tag cut 2026-06-30T23:41:13Z, npm `dist-tag.next` pointer moved 2026-07-01T00:05:02Z](https://www.npmjs.com/package/next) — 22 commits since [canary.71](https://github.com/vercel/next.js/compare/v16.3.0-canary.71...v16.3.0-canary.72); the **17 in-flight material PRs previously documented as `will be in canary.72+`** are now officially shipped). **16.3.0-canary.73 SHIPPED (July 1, 2026 — npm `canary` dist-tag pointer moved 2026-07-01T16:35Z, tag cut 2026-07-01T16:33:19Z, branch HEAD `c5645f69`, 17 commits since canary.72 — arrived 6–7 hours earlier than the 24h cadence predicted):** the 5 in-flight commits from the previous cron's coverage all shipped. Full canary.72 → canary.73 PR-by-PR diff at [compare/v16.3.0-canary.72...v16.3.0-canary.73](https://github.com/vercel/next.js/compare/v16.3.0-canary.72...v16.3.0-canary.73). FIVE new material PRs landed on the canary branch after the 12:04 UTC coverage and are now officially in canary.73: **(L) [PR #95151](https://github.com/vercel/next.js/pull/95151) — `[PP] Validate Shell prefetches (except gSP)`** by Janka Uryga, merged 2026-07-01T04:36:41Z — instant validation for `partialPrefetching: true` (or per-segment `prefetch = 'partial'` / `'unstable_eager'`) routes, where `<Link>` prefetches an App Shell that cannot access link data. New `DynamicHoleKind.Link = 1` in `packages/next/src/server/app-render/dynamic-rendering.ts` (renumbers `Runtime` 1→2 and `Dynamic` 2→3); new stage `ShellRuntime` added between `Static` and `Runtime` in `instant-validation.tsx`'s `SEGMENT_STAGE_ORDER`; new error builders `createLinkBodyErrorInNavigation` (error 1390), `createLinkMetadataError` (error 1391), `createLinkViewportError` (error 1392) in `packages/next/src/server/app-render/blocking-route-messages.ts`; new entries in `packages/next/errors.json`. Detection: if a hole is present in `ShellRuntime` but disappears in `Runtime`, it's link data (link data can resolve in Runtime but not in App Shell). 21 new test fixtures in `test/e2e/app-dir/instant-validation/app/shells/(default)/` (8 valid + 6 invalid route combos × instant config matrix); plus new `valid-session-only` / `valid-session-with-dynamic` fixtures prove session data is allowed outside Suspense in App Shells. **Known limitation (author-noted):** uses chunks from the dev render that resolve static params in Static stage, so static params are incorrectly included in ShellRuntime and don't trigger link-data errors — fixed by follow-up PR #94595. **(M) [PR #94595](https://github.com/vercel/next.js/pull/94595) — `[PP] Instant validation - error for unguarded static params`** by Janka Uryga, merged 2026-07-01T06:59:43Z — follow-up to #95151 extending link-data detection to `generateStaticParams`. Static params resolve in `Static` (for HTML-shell validation, initial-load path) vs `ShellRuntime` (for client-nav on `partialPrefetching: true`) via the new `requestStore.needsSessionShell` flag (DEV-only, set in three places in `app-render.tsx`). Three params utilities moved from inline definitions in `params.ts` into new `packages/next/src/server/lib/params-utils.ts` (`isEmptyParams`, `hasFallbackRouteParams`, `allParamsAreRootParams` + new `hasNonRootStaticParams`); `app-render.tsx` calls `hasNonRootStaticParams` to decide whether to skip the secondary render (optimization when no static params exist). **Known limitation (author-noted):** pre-existing `fallbackParams` bug in build validation (two tests marked `// TODO(app-shells): missing fallback params in build validation`); also doesn't touch `environmentName` (deliberately, follow-up PR coming). **(N) [#95336](https://github.com/vercel/next.js/pull/95336)** revert of test runner migration** — CI only, no user-facing impact. **(O) [#95106](https://github.com/vercel/next.js/pull/95106)** refactor: split out validateStaticShell** — internal refactor. **(P) [#95332](https://github.com/vercel/next.js/pull/95332)** `[ci] Move some test suites back to x86-64 runners`** — reverted by #95336, net no-op. These are the five in-flight commits from the previous cron's coverage — all now officially shipped in canary.73. **Plus FIVE NEW material PRs that landed in canary.73 after the previous cron's coverage (12:04 UTC):** **(Q) [PR #95314](https://github.com/vercel/next.js/pull/95314) — `codemod: bump eslint alongside eslint-config-next`** by Aurora Scharff, merged 2026-07-01T14:25:47Z — follow-up to PR #95312 (the `--yes` non-interactive flag). When the codemod bumps the installed `eslint-config-next` to the target version, it now also bumps `eslint` to a version that satisfies the new `eslint-config-next`'s peer dependency (reads the package's `peerDependencies.eslint` range and resolves the highest matching `eslint@latest` from the registry). Without this, default CNA-15 projects hit `ERESOLVE` from `eslint-config-next`'s new peer dep and the codemod loop fails. Tested against the `cna-15.0.0-app` fixture; same agent-loop motivation as #95312. Full details in setup.md → `@next/codemod upgrade` also bumps eslint. **(R) [PR #95309](https://github.com/vercel/next.js/pull/95309) — `Rename Copy as prompt to Copy prompt, convert FixOption to FixCard`** by Aurora Scharff, merged 2026-07-01T14:34:46Z — public dev-overlay UI rename. The button in Instant Insights is now **Copy prompt** (was `Copy as prompt`); the underlying fix-card data model renames `FixOption` → `FixCard` and the grid `FixOptionsList` → `FixCardGrid`. The skill's existing `Copy as prompt` references in patterns.md and the AI Improvements section of setup.md are now out of date — patterns.md's section heading + body was updated in this commit. Full details in patterns.md → Instant Insights / "Copy prompt" rename. **(S) [PR #95317](https://github.com/vercel/next.js/pull/95317) — `Fix early OTel proxy tracers in Cache Components prerenders`** by Jiwon Choi, merged 2026-07-01T16:14:49Z — silent dev-only corruption when Cache Components (`cacheComponents: true`) routes use OpenTelemetry: `Error: encountered unstable value in proxy tracer during prerender` was thrown mid-prerender when an OTel proxy tracer was installed before `workUnitStore` was fully set up. Closes issue #94753. Fix is to defer proxy-tracer creation until the OTel span is actually started (guarded by `workUnitStore !== null`). Full details in security.md → OTel proxy tracer Cache Components fix. **(T) [PR #95329](https://github.com/vercel/next.js/pull/95329) — `Fix Navigation Inspector in Safari`** by Sam Selikoff, merged 2026-07-01T14:34:25Z — Safari WebKit couldn't render the Navigation Inspector overlay because it didn't recognize the Chrome-`focus()`-then-screenshot sequence. Inspector now works in Safari too. Full details in performance.md → Navigation Inspector Safari fix. **(U) [PR #95311](https://github.com/vercel/next.js/pull/95311) — `docs: recommend explicit cacheLife and clarify overriding built-in cache profiles`** by Joseph, merged 2026-07-01T13:09:25Z — docs-only clarification in the caching guide: explicit `cacheLife('profile-name')` is now recommended over relying on the default `default-profile`; built-in profiles (`default-profile`, `hours`, `days`, `weeks`, `max`) can be overridden by exporting `cacheLife` from a file with the same name. Full details in patterns.md → cacheLife profile recommendations. The five non-material commits in canary.73 (test infrastructure, gitignore, CI workflow) are skipped. **Plus [React canary `19.3.0-canary-ec0fca31-20260701`](https://www.npmjs.com/package/react)** (cut 2026-07-01T16:30:24Z, replaces canary `e2731312-20260630`) — 5 commits ahead of the previous canary, one of which is material: **[React PR #36690](https://github.com/facebook/react/pull/36690) `Add parentEnter/parentExit props to ViewTransition`** by Jack Pope, merged 2026-07-01T16:16:48Z — new experimental feature for orchestrating parent-level view transitions alongside child element transitions. The new `parentEnter` / `parentExit` props (on `<ViewTransition>`) take `className` / `name` arrays and apply a view transition at the *parent container* level when any of its children transition. Gated behind the `enableViewTransitionParentEnterExit` feature flag in React; set in `react/src/ReactFeatureFlags.js` (default off). Use case: animate the page chrome (header, side nav) when a child component cross-fades, so the chrome transitions with the child rather than staying still underneath. Full details in patterns.md → ViewTransition parentEnter/parentExit. The other 4 React commits in this canary are devtools-facade / chrome-devtools-mcp E2E (non-material for the skill). **canary.73 is now the latest canary** (17 commits vs canary.72, see [compare/v16.3.0-canary.72...v16.3.0-canary.73](https://github.com/vercel/next.js/compare/v16.3.0-canary.72...v16.3.0-canary.73)). The 17 commits since canary.70 split into **eight material user-facing changes** (six Next.js canary-branch PRs + one Turbopack PR + one React canary PR that became the new canary-`92f4fda3-20260629`): **(1) fix: preserve repeated search params in client page segment cache keys** ([#94863](https://github.com/vercel/next.js/pulls/94863) by icyJoseph, merged June 29, 2026 at 11:54:29Z — silent stale-UI bug where `Object.fromEntries(new URLSearchParams(search))` collapsed `?foo=bar&foo=baz` to `{foo: ‘baz’}` in the client page segment cache key, causing cache collisions between `?color=red&color=blue` and `?color=blue`; affected `<Link>`, `router.replace`, `<form>` (next/form) soft-nav with repeated keys; fixed in `ppr-navigations.ts` + `segment-cache/scheduler.ts` + `route-params.ts` by switching to the existing `urlSearchParamsToParsedUrlQuery` helper which preserves repeated params as arrays; fixes issues [#92787](https://github.com/vercel/next.js/issues/92787) (filed against next 16.2.1-canary.38, April 14), [#93104](https://github.com/vercel/next.js/issues/93104) (filed against stable, April 21), and [#94821](https://github.com/vercel/next.js/issues/94821) (filed against canary.51, June 15 — next/form submission case); new e2e test at `test/e2e/app-dir/multi-value-search-params-stale/`; no API change, no config change, no opt-in flag; single-value URLs unaffected; **upgrade to canary.71+ or 16.3.0 stable to get the fix**; full details in routing.md); **(2) fix: client nav URL bar stuck on original URL after middleware redirect + rewrite-to-dynamic-route** ([#95207](https://github.com/vercel/next.js/pulls/95207) by acdlite / Andrew Clark, merged June 29, 2026 at 17:00:28Z — silent stale-URL bug where a click on `<Link href="/a">` with a proxy/middleware redirect `/a → /` AND rewrite `/ → /a` (where `/a` is a dynamic route reading `await params`) rendered the correct page content but left `window.location.href` stuck on `/a` instead of `/`; only fires on soft-nav, hard-nav was always correct via the server resolver; root cause was the optimistic-URL-commit in the Instant Navigations reducer — the prediction was committed up front and the resolved RSC payload (after the redirect) was treated as confirmation of the prediction when the redirect target had the same shape as the prediction; fix invalidates the prediction on a redirect discovered during navigation and re-resolves the route before the address bar is shown; touches `ppr-navigations.ts` + `reducers/server-patch-reducer.ts` + `router-reducer-types.ts`; new e2e test at `test/e2e/app-dir/redirect-rewrite-dynamic/`; **fixes [issue #95195](https://github.com/vercel/next.js/issues/95195) by amannn against 16.3.0-preview.5** with a [public Playwright reproduction repo](https://github.com/amannn/nextjs-16-3-bug-repro-redirect); full details in routing.md); **(3) Turbopack `experimental.cssChunking: ‘graph’` config schema breaking change** ([#95088](https://github.com/vercel/next.js/pull/95088) by sokra / Tobias Koppers, merged June 29, 2026 at 15:53:24Z — `moduleFactorCost` option REMOVED, replaced by `weightDistribution`; new per-group cost formula `chunk_group_weight * (chunk_size + request_cost)` where `chunk_group_weight = group_total_size ^ (-weightDistribution)`; defaults retuned: `requestCost` `20000` → `100000` bytes, `weightDistribution` default `0.1`; old `{ type: ‘graph’, requestCost, moduleFactorCost }` config throws a schema error in canary.71+ — string form `‘graph’` is unchanged and uses the new defaults automatically; full details in performance.md). **Plus [React canary `19.3.0-canary-92f4fda3-20260629`](https://www.npmjs.com/package/react) (cut 2026-06-29T17:11:25Z, replaces canary `68631c04-20260626`)** — 3 commits ahead of the previous canary, one of which is material. *(As of 2026-06-30T16:54:01Z, the React canary SHA has moved forward to `19.3.0-canary-e2731312-20260630` (replaces `92f4fda3-20260629`). The new canary is non-material for the skill — its 6 commits are devtools-facade work and a chrome-devtools-mcp integration; no new APIs or fixes relevant to documented patterns.)* **[React PR #36851](https://github.com/facebook/react/pull/36851) `[Float] Forward nonce option in ReactDOM.preloadModule()`** by udit / Udit Dewan, merged June 29, 2026 at 11:33:22Z — silent CSP violation where `ReactDOM.preloadModule(href, { nonce, as, ... })` accepted `nonce` in its public type but the implementation only forwarded `as`, `crossOrigin`, and `integrity` to the host dispatcher, dropping `nonce`; under strict `script-src ‘nonce-...’` CSP, the emitted `<link rel="modulepreload">` had no nonce and was blocked by the browser; `ReactDOM.preload()` and `ReactDOM.preinitModule()` already forwarded `nonce` correctly; 5 files, +37/-5 across `ReactDOMFloat.js`, `ReactDOMTypes.js`, `ReactFlightClientConfigDOM.js`, and the test suite; full details in security.md). The other 6 canary-branch commits since canary.70 are non-material (skill docs, CI, React upgrade bump, type improvement, internal Turbopack): `cc-adoption` skill tighten by Aurora Scharff ([#95225](https://github.com/vercel/next.js/pull/95225), docs-only), `unstable_rethrow` client-bundling fix by eps1lon ([#95196](https://github.com/vercel/next.js/pull/95196), internal bundling optimization, Closes #95189), Turbopack `server-reference-manifest` dedup by Tariqul ([#95242](https://github.com/vercel/next.js/pull/95242), internal bug fix), CC test command display fix by eps1lon ([#95267](https://github.com/vercel/next.js/pull/95267), CI), React canary bump in Next.js by Vercel Release Bot ([#95211](https://github.com/vercel/next.js/pull/95211), package.json only), `decodeReply` typing by mischnic ([#95263](https://github.com/vercel/next.js/pull/95263), type narrowing), `[changelog] Add 19.0.x, 19.1.x, 19.2.x patch releases` by eps1lon ([#36898](https://github.com/facebook/react/pull/36898), changelog only), `[test] coverage for multi-byte code units in stream APIs` by eps1lon ([#36900](https://github.com/facebook/react/pull/36900), test only); **16.3.0-canary.70** released June 28, 2026 (npm dist-tag pointer moved 2026-06-29T00:02:51Z) — only one new content commit ([#95232](https://github.com/vercel/next.js/pull/95232) by mischnic, `Turbopack: make hash_xxh3_hash128 return u128` — pure Rust internal type-narrowing refactor, zero user-facing impact, all files under `turbopack/crates/`); **16.3.0-canary.69** released June 26, 2026 ([`4b164d7`](https://github.com/vercel/next.js/releases/tag/v16.3.0-canary.69), 15 commits since canary.68) — **fix: preserve middleware rewrite query in Pages API routes** ([#94905](https://github.com/vercel/next.js/pulls/94905), fixes issue [#94647](https://github.com/vercel/next.js/issues/94647), June 10 — middleware rewrites from `/foo/bar?key=value` to `/api/proxy/bar?added=1&extra=2` were silently dropping the rewrite-added `added`/`extra` query params AND the destination catch-all `slug` param from `req.query` in Pages API handlers; regression from Next.js 14.2.35 introduced during the Pages API handler-interface migration, affected both webpack and Turbopack; `NextNodeServer.runApi` now forwards resolved `query` and `match.params` through the bundled handler context while preserving the original `req.url`; full details in routing.md), **hard-navigate to app routes shadowed by a pages dynamic route** ([#95185](https://github.com/vercel/next.js/pulls/95185), shipped in canary.69 — soft-nav misroute fix, full details in routing.md), dev overlay Copy-as-prompt body restructured into step-by-step checklist ([#95186](https://github.com/vercel/next.js/pull/95186)), docs: insights error pages restructure + link to preview docs ([#95193](https://github.com/vercel/next.js/pull/95193)), docs: reorder blocking-prerender-dynamic fixes to Stream/Cache/Block ([#95198](https://github.com/vercel/next.js/pull/95198)), `next-dev-loop` requires `agent-browser >= 0.31.1` ([#95209](https://github.com/vercel/next.js/pull/95209)), Turbopack Rust toolchain bump ([#95205](https://github.com/vercel/next.js/pull/95205)), docs: fix preview message typo ([#95050](https://github.com/vercel/next.js/pull/95050)), Turbopack internal: revert `final_read_hint` ([#95178](https://github.com/vercel/next.js/pull/95178)) — first canary cut after the preview.5 stabilisation, so #94905 + #95185 are the two user-facing changes; everything else is docs/dev-overlay/agent-skill/tooling/internal metrics)

also drops the `node-fetch` transitive dep in favor of native `fetch` ([#10905](https://github.com/shadcn-ui/ui/pull/10905)); **4.12.0 (June 26, 2026, same day as 4.11.1) ships the first phase of Chat Components** — `MessageScroller` / `Message` / `Bubble` / `Attachment` / `Marker` (each available in Base UI + Radix UI flavors), two new CSS utilities `scroll-fade` + `shimmer` (shipped via `shadcn/tailwind.css`, so any project initialized with `npx shadcn@latest init` gets them for free), and a brand-new npm package **`@shadcn/react@0.1.0`** — unstyled headless React primitives, first primitive is `@shadcn/react/message-scroller` with the same interaction logic the registry item wraps)

See setup.md → Vite 8.1.2 for the long-form patterns, the `tsconfig.json` `include` requirement for CSS/Sass files, and the 8.1.1/8.1.2/es-module-lexer migration matrix)

- **@playwright/test 1.61.1** (E2E — **1.61.0 (June 15, 2026)** shipped WebAuthn passkeys virtual authenticator via `browserContext.credentials.create/install`, `page.localStorage` / `page.sessionStorage` (origin-aware, replaces `page.evaluate('localStorage…')` boilerplate), `apiResponse.securityDetails()` / `serverAddr()` network parity with browser responses, `testOptions.video` modes parity with trace (`'on-all-retries'`, `'retain-on-first-failure'`, `'retain-on-failure-and-retries'`), `expect.soft.poll(...)`, `fullConfig.argv`, `fullConfig.failOnFlakyTests`, `testInfo.errors` AggregateError sub-entries, `-G` shorthand for `--grep-invert`, HAR + trace now include WebSocket frames, Ubuntu 26.04 host support, browsers Chromium 149.0.7827.55 / Firefox 151.0 / WebKit 26.5; **1.61.1 (June 23, 2026)** fixed five 1.61 regressions — `expect.extend` matcher-name shadowing built-ins, UI-mode API request byte count mismatch, trace-viewer WebSocket timestamps scaled by 1/1000, sync ESM loader crash on Node 22.15, pnpm workspace symlink `.ts` subpath resolution)

## Pro Tips

- Use `create-next-app --typescript --tailwind --eslint --app --src-dir --import-alias "@/*"` for a fully typed Next.js project
- Run `npx tsc --noEmit` to type-check without building
- Use `shadcn@latest add <component>` to add components — they live in your codebase, not node_modules
- Use `next.config.ts` for all Next.js configuration (not `.js`)
- Use `react-hook-form` + `zod` for all forms — never manage form state with useState
- Enable React Compiler in `next.config.ts` with `reactCompiler: true` — eliminates most `useMemo`/`useCallback` manually
- Use Biome (`npx biome check`) for linting — 10–100x faster than ESLint
- Run `npx biome migrate --write` after every Biome upgrade to migrate your config for breaking changes
- Use `use cache` for all server-side data fetching in Next.js 16 — it's the explicit opt-in caching model

Start with `setup.md` to initialize a project, then `components.md` for shadcn/ui patterns.

## Next.js 16 Migration Notes

Key breaking changes from Next.js 15 → 16:

| Change | Impact |
|---|---|
| **`use cache` directive** replaces `unstable_cache` | New explicit caching API — mark data functions with `use cache` + `cacheTag` |
| **Implicit caching removed** — everything is dynamic by default | Old `fetch` caching patterns deprecated; use `use cache` explicitly |
| **PPR (Partial Prerendering) stable** — `cacheComponents: true` | Static shell + streaming dynamic content; no longer experimental |
| **Router scroll optimization** enabled by default | Previously scroll was reset on navigation; now preserved by default |
| Flat config default in `@next/eslint-plugin-next` | ESLint config format change |
| Deprecated `.turbo` config object removed | Use `turbopack` key in `next.config.ts` instead |
| `publicRuntimeConfig` / `serverRuntimeConfig` removed | Use environment variables directly |
| **`next lint` removed** | Use Biome (`npx biome check`) or ESLint (`npx eslint .`) directly |
| **`middleware.ts` deprecated → `proxy.ts`** | `middleware` export renamed to `proxy`; must be `async`; `matcher` moved to `next.config.ts` or exported from `proxy.ts` |

**Upgrade:** `npx @next/codemod@canary upgrade latest` (Next.js 16.2+) — automated upgrade CLI that handles codemods, config migrations, and breaking changes in one command. **In canary.72+ (PR #95312, June 30, 2026) accepts `-y, --yes` for agents/CI** — auto-enabled when stdin is not a TTY so AI coding agents no longer hang on the recommended-codemods multiselect. Alternatively `npx next upgrade` (interactive, Next.js 16.1+) or `npm install next@latest`.

## 1.4.77 — 2026-07-21T18:03Z

**JULY 2026 SECURITY RELEASE — ALL 9 CVEs NOW DOCUMENTED.** The July 21, 2026 security release shipped at ~16:00 UTC (16.2.11 / 15.5.21). Full CVE table added to `security.md`: 4 High (DoS via Server Actions, Turbopack middleware bypass with single i18n locale, SSRF via rewrites, SSRF via Server Actions on custom servers) + 5 Medium (Image Optimization SVG DoS, unbounded Edge payload, Server Function endpoint disclosure, cache confusion ×2). The live status checkpoints (T-24h through T+6h) removed and replaced with final resolution. `api.md` gained "Server Action Security Hardening (canary.92)" section covering 7 PRs: bodySizeLimit Edge enforcement, correct origin for custom-server redirects, exotic rewrite param encoding, server reference ID validation (closes CVE-2026-64643), basePath preservation in redirects, and two fetch cache key fixes (effective-request keying + byte-exact binary body hashing). `deployment.md` gained "July 2026 Security Releases" section documenting 16.2.11 (Active LTS) + 15.5.21 (Maintenance LTS) + canary.92 upgrade path, post-upgrade checklist, and the monthly cadence reminder (next: August 20, 2026). canary.92 non-security items: Turbopack middleware i18n single-locale fix, MPA form validation perf, SVG detectContentType perf, instant validation navigation fix, `use cache` error handling, docs AI agents guide expansion. React 19.2.8 (2026-07-21) — performance improvements when decoding RSC (PR #37087, eps1lon). TanStack Query 5.101.4 (2026-07-21) — patch on query-core 5.101.4.

Sources:
- [Next.js blog: July 2026 Security Release](https://nextjs.org/blog/july-2026-security-release)
